## 入宫语义精确绑定Schema

```text
S07_EFFECTIVE_SCOPE_ENTITY_FIELDS=EFFECTIVE_SCOPE_ID|SOURCE_ROOT_ATOM_ID|PALACE_OBJECT_ID|STRUCTURE_PHYSICAL_KEY|INTERPRETATION_CONTEXT_KEY|SUBJECT_IDENTITY_ANCHOR_ID|SUBJECT_ENTITY_TYPE|PERSON_ROLE_CANDIDATE_IDS|PALACE_COORDINATE_CONTEXT_ID|TIME_LAYER|MATCHED_CONDITION_IDS|UNMATCHED_CONDITION_IDS|OPERATOR_OCCURRENCE_IDS|SOURCE_SPAN_IDS|RECORD_HASH|STATUS
S07_RESULT_TERM_OCCURRENCE_FIELDS=RESULT_TERM_OCCURRENCE_ID|SOURCE_ROOT_ATOM_ID|EFFECTIVE_SCOPE_ID|RAW_RESULT_ALIAS|CANONICAL_MODERN_RESULT_CONCEPT|RESULT_TERM_CLASS|SUBJECT_IDENTITY_ANCHOR_ID|SUBJECT_ENTITY_TYPE|PERSON_ROLE_ID|ACTION_OCCURRENCE_ID|OBJECT_OCCURRENCE_ID|POLARITY_STATUS|LOGICAL_BRANCH_ID|RESULT_OCCURRENCE_ROLE|REQUIRED_ACTION_CHAIN_CLASS|REQUIRED_ENDPOINT_CHAIN_CLASS|PROFESSIONAL_BOUNDARY_TYPE|BASE_CONTRIBUTION|RECORD_HASH|STATUS
S07_ANTECEDENT_REFERENCE_FIELDS=REFERENCE_ENTITY_ID|SOURCE_ROOT_ATOM_ID|RAW_REFERENCE_TEXT|ANTECEDENT_ENTITY_IDS|TARGET_TYPE|REFERENCE_DIRECTION|TARGET_ORDER|REFERENCE_SCOPE|RESOLUTION_STATUS|UNKNOWN_IDS|CONFLICT_IDS|BASE_CONTRIBUTION|RECORD_HASH|STATUS
S07_TO_S17_HANDOFF_FIELDS=HANDOFF_ID|CASE_ID|QUESTION_ID|OPTION_ID|CLAIM_ID|ATOM_ID|SOURCE_ROOT_ATOM_IDS|STRUCTURE_PHYSICAL_KEYS|INTERPRETATION_CONTEXT_KEYS|PHYSICAL_EVIDENCE_KEYS|EVENT_ATOM_KEY|SUBJECT_IDENTITY_ANCHOR_ID|SUBJECT_ENTITY_TYPE|PERSON_ROLE_CANDIDATE_IDS|PALACE_COORDINATE_CONTEXT_ID|RESULT_TERM_OCCURRENCE_IDS|OPERATOR_OCCURRENCE_IDS|ANTECEDENT_REFERENCE_IDS|UNKNOWN_IDS|CONFLICT_IDS|RECORD_HASH|STATUS
```

同一原子中多个结果词、多个主体或混合极性必须分别保存；不得以单一`RESULT_TERM`覆盖。人物候选集合只用于候选路由，S17锁定后必须回写唯一角色或保留UNKNOWN／CONFLICT。

## 辅佐煞杂曜来源入宫投影

```text
S07_AUX_PROJECTION_RELATION_BUNDLE_FIELDS=SOURCE_HEADING_SCOPE_IDS|SOURCE_RELATION_IDS|RELATION_TARGET_CLAUSE_IDS|RELATION_RESOLUTION_STATUS
AUX_PROJECTION_RELATION_TARGET_REQUIRED=YES
AUX_PROJECTION_RESULT_OCCURRENCE_ROLE_REQUIRED=YES
```

投影仅复制必要上下文并固定零第二贡献；关系目标、父标题链和结果词角色缺一时不得直接交接。


### 辅佐来源词义投影消歧

```text
AUX_OCCUPATIONAL_OBJECT_MARRIAGE_ROUTE_PERMISSION=NO
AUX_TECHNICAL_STAGE_HEALTH_ROUTE_PERMISSION=NO
AUX_FALSE_OPERATOR_PROJECTION_PERMISSION=NO
AUX_GENERAL_BENEFIT_FINANCE_ROUTE_PERMISSION=CONTEXT_REQUIRED
```

投影必须继承S05的词义、动作角色和逻辑算子消歧；“结婚仪注行业”不得进入婚姻入宫语义，“病死绝之乡”不得进入健康入宫语义，“是非”不得建立否定，“背后”不得建立时间顺序。

```text
SOURCE_FAMILY_ID=S05-SRC-AUX-MISC-NATURE
SOURCE_FILE_SHA256=46784471ef6a0673bbca8e809124c14c143dc24d6ea7166b689f5df87da7a610
S07_AUX_PROJECTION_COUNT=338
PROJECTION_PRIMARY_AUTHORITY=S05
PROJECTION_BASE_CONTRIBUTION=ZERO
FULL_SOURCE_SCAN_PERMISSION=NO
DIRECT_LOOKUP_TEXT_EMBEDDED=YES
```

S07只解释目标星曜、组合或流曜进入指定宫位和位置关系后的语义。稳定本性、组合成立门仍回查S05；时间层回查S10；结果词只作候选并回流S17。
